- **pr-distill**: 1. **Heuristics First**: Run heuristic pattern matching before AI analysis. Heuristics are fast and deterministic. 2. **Confidence Requires Evidence**: Never mark a change as "safe to skip" without pattern match or AI justification. 3. **Surface Uncertainty**: Low-confidence cases → categorize as "uncertain". Humans decide ambiguous cases. 4. **Preserve Context**: Include enough diff context for r ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/pr-distill.md) · ★13)
- **pr-distill-bless**: 1. **User Confirmation Required**: Never bless patterns automatically. User must explicitly invoke this command. 2. **Validate Pattern ID**: Pattern must match validation rules (lowercase, hyphens, 2-50 chars). 3. **Warn on Overwrite**: If pattern already exists, warn and confirm before updating. 4. **Persistence Is Immediate**: Once blessed, pattern affects all future distillations in this projec ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/pr-distill-bless.md) · ★13)
